1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the universe object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 271. The moduli space has dimension at most 930 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 271 + O(1/n^930). Summing over all modes and applying the universe convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
